========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Settings.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';
  import { auth } from '../stores/auth';
  import { cardTheme, CARD_THEMES } from '../stores/cardTheme';

  export let onBack: () => void = () => {};

  let sound = true;
  let music = false;
  let haptics = true;
  let animations = true;
  let graphics: 'ultra' | 'high' | 'medium' | 'performance' = 'high';
  let autoMuck = true;
  let fourColor = false;
  let betSlider = true;
  let autoPostBlinds = true;

  onMount(() => {
    try {
      const saved = localStorage.getItem('settings');
      if (saved) {
        const s = JSON.parse(saved);
        sound = s.sound ?? true;
        music = s.music ?? false;
        haptics = s.haptics ?? true;
        animations = s.animations ?? true;
        graphics = s.graphics ?? 'high';
        autoMuck = s.autoMuck ?? true;
        fourColor = s.fourColor ?? false;
        betSlider = s.betSlider ?? true;
        autoPostBlinds = s.autoPostBlinds ?? true;
      }
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  });

  function save() {
    try {
      localStorage.setItem(
        'settings',
        JSON.stringify({
          sound, music, haptics, animations, graphics,
          autoMuck, fourColor, betSlider, autoPostBlinds,
        }),
      );
    } catch {
      /* ignore */
    }
  }

  $: if (typeof window !== 'undefined') save();
</script>

<div class="px-4 pb-28">
  <TopBar />
  <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mt-3 mb-4">
    <button
      on:click={onBack}
      aria-label="Back"
      class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95 transition"
      style="
        background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(30,58,138,0.4), rgba(15,31,58,0.7));
        border: 1.5px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.3);
      "
    >
      <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"
           stroke="white" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round">
        <path d="M15 18l-6-6 6-6" />
      </svg>
    </button>
    <h1 class="text-xl font-extrabold flex-1">⚙️ Settings</h1>
  </div>

  <!-- ============ CARD THEME ============ -->
  <h2 class="t-label text-gold/60 mb-2 px-1">Card Theme</h2>
  <div
    class="rounded-2xl p-1.5 mb-4"
    style="
      background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9));
      border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);
      box-shadow: var(--shadow-card), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.04);
    "
  >
    {#each CARD_THEMES as t}
      <button
        on:click={() => cardTheme.set(t.id)}
        class="w-full flex items-center gap-3 px-3 py-2.5 rounded-xl active:scale-[0.98] transition-all text-left"
        style="
          background: {$cardTheme === t.id
            ? 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(212,175,55,0.2), rgba(168,122,21,0.08))'
            : 'transparent'};
          border: 1.5px solid {$cardTheme === t.id
            ? 'rgba(212,175,55,0.5)'
            : 'transparent'};
          { $cardTheme === t.id
            ? 'box-shadow: 0 0 12px rgba(212,175,55,0.25);'
            : ''}
        "
      >
        <!-- Radio circle -->
        <div
          class="w-5 h-5 rounded-full border-2 flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"
          style="
            border-color: {$cardTheme === t.id ? '#d4af37' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.25)'};
            background: {$cardTheme === t.id ? 'rgba(212,175,55,0.15)' : 'transparent'};
          "
        >
          {#if $cardTheme === t.id}
            <div class="w-2.5 h-2.5 rounded-full" style="background: #d4af37;"></div>
          {/if}
        </div>

        <!-- Name -->
        <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
          <p
            class="text-[13px] font-extrabold leading-tight"
            style="color: {$cardTheme === t.id ? '#f4e5a8' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)'};"
          >
            {t.name}
          </p>
          <p class="text-[10px] text-white/40 leading-tight mt-0.5">
            {t.nameFa} · {t.desc}
          </p>
        </div>

        <!-- Color swatches -->
        <div class="flex gap-1 shrink-0">
          {#each t.previewColors as c}
            <div
              class="w-3 h-3 rounded-full"
              style="
                background: {c};
                box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 0.5px rgba(255,255,255,0.3);
              "
            ></div>
          {/each}
        </div>
      </button>
    {/each}
  </div>

  <!-- ============ AUDIO ============ -->
  <h2 class="t-label text-white/40 mb-2 px-1">Audio</h2>
  <div class="card !p-0 mb-4 overflow-hidden">

<script lang="ts">
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';

  export let onBack: () => void = () => {};

  type Category = 'all' | 'chips' | 'cards' | 'frames' | 'table';
  type Rarity = 'common' | 'rare' | 'legendary' | 'immortal' | 'arcana' | 'ton';

  let activeCategory: Category = 'all';
  let rarityFilter: Rarity | 'all' = 'all';
  let priceFilter: 'all' | 'low' | 'high' = 'all';

  interface Item {
    id: string;
    name: string;
    category: Category;
    rarity: Rarity;
    priceUsd: number;
    priceTon: number;
    image: string;
    imageBg: string;
    label: string;
  }

  const items: Item[] = [
    { id: 'chip_blue',   name: 'Blue Chip Pack',   category: 'chips',  rarity: 'common',    priceUsd: 0.99,  priceTon: 0.25,  image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_blue',   label: 'Common' },
    { id: 'chip_green',  name: 'Green Chip Pack',  category: 'chips',  rarity: 'rare',      priceUsd: 4.99,  priceTon: 1.25,  image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_green',  label: 'Rare' },
    { id: 'chip_gold',   name: 'Gold Chip Pack',   category: 'chips',  rarity: 'legendary', priceUsd: 19.99, priceTon: 5.00,  image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_gold',   label: 'Legendary' },
    { id: 'chip_purple', name: 'Purple Chip Pack', category: 'chips',  rarity: 'immortal',  priceUsd: 49.99, priceTon: 12.50, image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_purple', label: 'Immortal' },
    { id: 'chip_red',    name: 'Red Chip Pack',    category: 'chips',  rarity: 'arcana',    priceUsd: 99.99, priceTon: 25.00, image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_red',    label: 'Arcana' },
    { id: 'chip_ton',    name: 'TON Chip Pack',    category: 'chips',  rarity: 'ton',       priceUsd: 99.99, priceTon: 25.00, image: '/chips-set-hq.png', imageBg: 'chip_ton',    label: 'TON' },
    { id: 'card_royal',  name: 'Royal Deck',       category: 'cards',  rarity: 'legendary', priceUsd: 4.99,  priceTon: 1.25,  image: '/card-back-royal.png',   imageBg: 'card_royal',   label: 'Legendary' },
    { id: 'card_classic',name: 'Classic Deck',     category: 'cards',  rarity: 'rare',      priceUsd: 2.99,  priceTon: 0.75,  image: '/card-back-classic.png', imageBg: 'card_classic', label: 'Rare' },
    { id: 'card_dark',   name: 'Dark Deck',        category: 'cards',  rarity: 'immortal',  priceUsd: 6.99,  priceTon: 1.75,  image: '/card-back-dark.png',    imageBg: 'card_dark',    label: 'Immortal' },
    { id: 'frame_royal', name: 'Royal Frame',      category: 'frames', rarity: 'legendary', priceUsd: 9.99,  priceTon: 2.50,  image: '/frame-royal.png',       imageBg: 'frame_royal',  label: 'Legendary' },
    { id: 'frame_epic',  name: 'Epic Frame',       category: 'frames', rarity: 'rare',      priceUsd: 3.99,  priceTon: 1.00,  image: '/frame-epic.png',        imageBg: 'frame_epic',   label: 'Rare' },
    { id: 'table_royal', name: 'Royal Table',      category: 'table',  rarity: 'immortal',  priceUsd: 12.99, priceTon: 3.25,  image: '/table-top.png',         imageBg: 'table_royal',  label: 'Immortal' },
  ];

  const categories: { key: Category; label: string }[] = [
    { key: 'all', label: 'All' },
    { key: 'chips', label: 'Chips' },
    { key: 'cards', label: 'Cards' },
    { key: 'frames', label: 'Frames' },
    { key: 'table', label: 'Table' },
  ];

  const rarityColors: Record<string, string> = {
    common: '#94a3b8',
    rare: '#3b82f6',
    legendary: '#d4af37',
    immortal: '#8b5cf6',
    arcana: '#ef4444',
    ton: '#22d3ee',
  };

  // Chip background positions (for chips-set-hq.png 4×3 grid)
  const chipBg: Record<string, string> = {
    chip_blue:   '0% 0%',    // 1 chip
    chip_green:  '33.33% 0%', // 5 chip
    chip_gold:   '66.66% 0%', // 25 chip
    chip_purple: '100% 0%',  // 100 chip
    chip_red:    '0% 50%',   // 500 chip
    chip_ton:    '33.33% 50%', // 1K chip
  };

  $: filtered = items.filter((i) => {
    if (activeCategory !== 'all' && i.category !== activeCategory) return false;
    if (rarityFilter !== 'all' && i.rarity !== rarityFilter) return false;
    if (priceFilter === 'low' && i.priceUsd > 5) return false;
    if (priceFilter === 'high' && i.priceUsd <= 5) return false;
    return true;
  });

  let balanceUsd = 2840;
  let balanceTon = 1.25;

  function getImageStyle(item: Item): string {
    if (item.category === 'chips') {
      const pos = chipBg[item.imageBg] || '0% 0%';
      return `
        background-image: url('${item.image}');
        background-size: 400% 300%;
        background-position: ${pos};
        background-repeat: no-repeat;
      `;
    }
    if (item.category === 'cards') {
      return `
        background-image: url('${item.image}');
        background-size: 55% auto;
        background-position: center;
        background-repeat: no-repeat;
        border-radius: 8px;
      `;
    }
    if (item.category === 'table') {
      return `
        background-image: url('${item.image}');
        background-size: 95% auto;
        background-position: center;
        background-repeat: no-repeat;
      `;
    }
    // frames
    return `
      background-image: url('${item.image}');
      background-size: 70% auto;
      background-position: center;
      background-repeat: no-repeat;
    `;
  }
</script>

      <span class="text-[13px] font-extrabold text-white tabular-nums">{balanceTon}</span>

    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- CATEGORIES -->
  <div class="flex gap-1.5 mb-3 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pb-1 px-4">
    {#each categories as c}
      <button
        on:click={() => (activeCategory = c.key)}
        class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap active:scale-95 transition-all uppercase tracking-wider"
        style="
          background: {activeCategory === c.key
            ? 'linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8 0%, #d4af37 45%, #a87a15 100%)'
            : 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9))'};
          color: {activeCategory === c.key ? '#0a0800' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)'};
          border: 1px solid {activeCategory === c.key ? 'transparent' : 'rgba(212,175,55,0.15)'};
          {activeCategory === c.key ? 'box-shadow: 0 2px 0 #7a560a, inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,235,170,0.4);' : ''}
        "
      >
        {c.label}
      </button>
    {/each}
  </div>

  <!-- FILTERS -->
  <div class="flex gap-2 mb-4 px-4">
    <div class="relative flex-1">
      <select bind:value={rarityFilter}
              class="w-full rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-white outline-none appearance-none pr-8"
              style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.2);">
        <option value="all">All Rarity</option>
        <option value="common">Common</option>
        <option value="rare">Rare</option>
        <option value="legendary">Legendary</option>
        <option value="immortal">Immortal</option>
        <option value="arcana">Arcana</option>
        <option value="ton">TON</option>
      </select>
      <svg class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" width="12" height="12" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#d4af37" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round">
        <path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6" />
      </svg>
    </div>

    <div class="relative flex-1">
      <select bind:value={priceFilter}
              class="w-full rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-white outline-none appearance-none pr-8"
              style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.2);">
        <option value="all">All Prices</option>
        <option value="low">Under $5</option>
        <option value="high">$5+</option>
      </select>
      <svg class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" width="12" height="12" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#d4af37" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round">
        <path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6" />
      </svg>
    </div>
  </div>

  <!-- ITEMS GRID -->
  <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 px-4">
    {#each filtered as item}
      {@const rc = rarityColors[item.rarity] || '#94a3b8'}
      <button
        class="rounded-2xl overflow-hidden active:scale-[0.97] transition-all text-left relative"

========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Splash.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';

  let visible = true;
  onMount(() => {
    const t = setTimeout(() => {
      visible = false;
      window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('splash-done'));
    }, 3000);
    return () => clearTimeout(t);
  });
</script>

<div
  class="relative min-h-screen w-full overflow-hidden flex flex-col items-center justify-center"
  style="display: {visible ? 'flex' : 'none'};"
>
  <!-- Background -->
  <div class="absolute inset-0" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, #070e1b 0%, #05080f 100%);"></div>

  <!-- Pattern -->

  ></div>

  <!-- Content -->

========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Table.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';
  import { api } from '../lib/api';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';

  export let initialVariant: string = 'all';
  export let initialMode: 'all' | 'free' | 'real' = 'all';
  export let onOpenTable: (id: number) => void = () => {};

  let tables: any[] = [];
  let loading = true;
  let filterMode: 'all' | 'free' | 'real' = initialMode;
  let filterVariant: string = initialVariant;

  onMount(async () => {
    const res = await api.tables();
    if (res.ok && res.data) tables = res.data.tables;
    loading = false;
  });

  $: filtered = tables.filter((tbl) => {
    const okMode = filterMode === 'all' || tbl.mode === filterMode;
    const okVariant = filterVariant === 'all' || tbl.variant === filterVariant;
    return okMode && okVariant;
  });

  // Determine chip icon based on blind level
  function chipColorForTable(tbl: any): string {
    const bb = Number(tbl.blinds?.bb || 0);
    if (tbl.mode === 'free') {
      if (bb <= 1) return '#94a3b8';
      if (bb <= 10) return '#3b82f6';
      return '#10b981';
    }
    if (bb <= 1) return '#f59e0b';
    if (bb <= 20) return '#dc2626';
    if (bb <= 100) return '#0a0a0a';
    if (bb <= 1000) return '#8b5cf6';
    return '#d4af37';
  }

  function variantLabel(v: string): string {
    if (v === 'holdem') return "Texas Hold'em";
    if (v === 'omaha4') return 'Omaha 4';
    if (v === 'omaha5') return 'Omaha 5';
    return v;
  }

  function formatBlinds(tbl: any): string {
    const sb = Number(tbl.blinds?.sb || 0);
    const bb = Number(tbl.blinds?.bb || 0);
    return `${sb} / ${bb}`;
  }

  function playersCount(tbl: any): string {
    const cur = tbl.live_players || 0;
    const max = tbl.maxSeats || tbl.seats || 9;
    return `${cur} / ${max}`;
  }
</script>

<div class="pb-44 lg:pb-8">
  <TopBar />

  <!-- HEADER -->
  <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mt-3 mb-4 px-4">
    <button
      aria-label="Back"
      class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95 transition"
      style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(30,58,138,0.4), rgba(15,31,58,0.7)); border: 1.5px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.3);"
    >
      <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="white" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round">
        <path d="M15 18l-6-6 6-6" />
      </svg>
    </button>
    <h1 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-white flex-1">Cash Games</h1>
  </div>

  <!-- VARIANT TABS -->
  <div class="flex gap-1.5 mb-3 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pb-1 px-4">
    {#each [
      { key: 'all', label: 'All' },
      { key: 'holdem', label: "Hold'em" },
      { key: 'omaha4', label: 'Omaha 4' },
      { key: 'omaha5', label: 'Omaha 5' },
    ] as v}
      <button
        on:click={() => (filterVariant = v.key)}
        class="px-4 py-2 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap active:scale-95 transition-all uppercase tracking-wider"
        style="
          background: {filterVariant === v.key
            ? 'linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8 0%, #d4af37 45%, #a87a15 100%)'
            : 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9))'};
          color: {filterVariant === v.key ? '#0a0800' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)'};
          border: 1px solid {filterVariant === v.key ? 'transparent' : 'rgba(212,175,55,0.15)'};
          {filterVariant === v.key ? 'box-shadow: 0 2px 0 #7a560a, inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,235,170,0.4);' : ''}
        "
      >
        {v.label}
      </button>
    {/each}
  </div>

  <!-- MODE FILTER -->
  <div class="flex gap-2 mb-4 px-4">
    <div class="relative flex-1">
      <select bind:value={filterMode}
              class="w-full rounded-xl px-3 py-2.5 text-[11px] font-bold text-white outline-none appearance-none pr-8"
              style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.2);">
        <option value="all">All Stakes</option>
        <option value="free">Free Only</option>
        <option value="real">Real Money</option>
      </select>
      <svg class="absolute right-3 top-1/2 -translate-y-1/2 pointer-events-none" width="12" height="12" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="#d4af37" stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round">
        <path d="M6 9l6 6 6-6" />
      </svg>
    </div>
    <button
      on:click={() => {}}
      aria-label="Refresh"
      class="w-10 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95"
      style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.2);"
    >
      <span class="text-sm">🔄</span>
    </button>
  </div>

  <!-- TABLE LIST -->
  {#if loading}
    <div class="space-y-2 px-4">
      {#each Array(4) as _}
        <div class="rounded-2xl p-3 h-[70px] shimmer"
             style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.6), rgba(11,19,36,0.8)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.1);"></div>
      {/each}
    </div>
  {:else if filtered.length === 0}
    <div class="rounded-2xl p-8 text-center mx-4"
         style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(7,14,27,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);">
      <div class="text-4xl mb-2">🃏</div>
      <p class="text-white/60 text-sm font-bold">No tables found</p>
      <p class="text-white/40 text-[11px] mt-1">Try a different filter</p>
    </div>
  {:else}
    <div class="space-y-2 px-4">
      {#each filtered as tbl}
        {@const chipColor = chipColorForTable(tbl)}
        {@const isFree = tbl.mode === 'free'}
        <button
          on:click={() => onOpenTable(tbl.id)}
          class="w-full text-left rounded-2xl active:scale-[0.98] transition-all relative overflow-hidden flex items-center gap-3 p-3"
          style="
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.85) 0%, rgba(7,14,27,0.95) 100%);
            border: 1.5px solid {isFree ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.2)' : 'rgba(212,175,55,0.25)'};
            box-shadow: 0 4px 14px rgba(0,0,0,0.5), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.04);
          "
        >
          <!-- Chip icon -->
          <div class="shrink-0 relative" style="width: 48px; height: 48px;">
            <!-- Outer metallic ring -->
            <div
              class="absolute inset-0 rounded-full"
              style="
                background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8 0%, #d4af37 45%, #a87a15 100%);
                box-shadow: 0 3px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.6), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.4);
              "
            ></div>
            <!-- Colored inner -->
            <div
              class="absolute rounded-full"
              style="
                inset: 4px;
                background: radial-gradient(circle at 40% 35%, {chipColor}, {chipColor}dd);
                box-shadow: inset 0 -3px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.4), inset 0 2px 4px rgba(255,255,255,0.15);
              "
            ></div>
            <!-- Inner white ring -->
            <div
              class="absolute rounded-full"
              style="
                inset: 9px;
                border: 1.5px dashed rgba(255,255,255,0.4);
              "
            ></div>
            <!-- Center value -->
            <div class="absolute inset-0 flex items-center justify-center">
              <span
                class="text-[11px] font-extrabold tabular-nums"
                style="
                  color: white;
                  text-shadow: 0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.8);
                "
              >
                ${Number(tbl.blinds?.bb || 0) >= 1000 ? (Number(tbl.blinds.bb) / 1000).toFixed(0) + 'K' : Number(tbl.blinds?.bb || 0)}
              </span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Info -->
          <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-2">
              <p class="text-[13px] font-extrabold text-white truncate">
                {variantLabel(tbl.variant)}
              </p>
              {#if isFree}
                <span
                  class="text-[8px] font-extrabold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full tracking-wider"
                  style="background: rgba(16,185,129,0.25); color: #6ee7b7; border: 1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.5);"
                >
                  FREE
                </span>
              {:else}
                <span
                  class="text-[8px] font-extrabold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full tracking-wider"
                  style="
                    background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8, #d4af37 45%, #a87a15);
                    color: #0a0800;
                    border: 1px solid rgba(255,235,170,0.5);
                  "
                >
                  REAL
                </span>
              {/if}
            </div>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-white/55 font-mono mt-0.5 tabular-nums">
              {formatBlinds(tbl)}
            </p>
          </div>

          <!-- Players count -->
          <div class="text-right shrink-0 mr-2">
            <p class="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">Seats</p>

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';
  import { api } from '../lib/api';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';

  export let initialVariant: string = 'all';
  export let initialMode: 'all' | 'free' | 'real' = 'all';
  export let onOpenTable: (id: number) => void = () => {};

  let tables: any[] = [];
  let loading = true;
  let filterMode: 'all' | 'free' | 'real' = initialMode;
  let filterVariant: string = initialVariant;

  onMount(async () => {
    const res = await api.tables();
    if (res.ok && res.data) tables = res.data.tables;
    loading = false;
  });

  $: filtered = tables.filter((tbl) => {
    const okMode = filterMode === 'all' || tbl.mode === filterMode;
    const okVariant = filterVariant === 'all' || tbl.variant === filterVariant;
    return okMode && okVariant;
  });

  function variantEmoji(v: string): string {
    if (v === 'holdem') return '♠️';
    if (v === 'omaha4') return '♦️';
    if (v === 'omaha5') return '♣️';
    return '🎴';
  }

  function variantLabel(v: string): string {
    if (v === 'holdem') return "Hold'em";
    if (v === 'omaha4') return 'Omaha 4';
    if (v === 'omaha5') return 'Omaha 5';
    return v;
  }

  function variantColor(v: string): string {
    if (v === 'holdem') return '#3b82f6';
    if (v === 'omaha4') return '#ef4444';
    if (v === 'omaha5') return '#10b981';
    return '#d4af37';
  }
</script>
