              <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mb-1">
                <p class="font-extrabold text-white text-sm truncate">
                  {table.name}
                </p>
                <span
                  class="text-[8px] font-extrabold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full shrink-0 tracking-wider"
                  style="
                    background: {isFree
                      ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.25)'
                      : 'linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8, #d4af37 45%, #a87a15)'};
                    color: {isFree ? '#6ee7b7' : '#0a0800'};
                    border: 1px solid {isFree
                      ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.5)'
                      : 'rgba(255,235,170,0.5)'};
                    box-shadow: {isFree ? 'none' : '0 1px 0 rgba(122,86,10,0.6)'};
                  "
                >
                  {isFree ? 'FREE' : 'REAL'}
                </span>
              </div>

              <p class="text-[11px] text-white/60 font-mono">
                {variantLabel(table.variant)} · <span class="text-cyan/80">${table.blinds.sb}/{table.blinds.bb}</span>
              </p>

              <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mt-1.5 text-[10px]">
                <span class="flex items-center gap-1 text-white/50">
                  <span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full" style="background: {accent}; box-shadow: 0 0 6px {accent};"></span>
                  {table.seats} seats
                </span>
                {#if table.mode === 'real' && table.minBuyIn > 0}
                  <span class="text-gold/80">
                    Min: <span class="font-bold">${table.minBuyIn}</span>
                  </span>
                {/if}
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Arrow -->
            <div
              class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center shrink-0"
              style="
                background: {isFree
                  ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.15)'
                  : 'rgba(212,175,55,0.15)'};
                border: 1px solid {isFree
                  ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.4)'
                  : 'rgba(212,175,55,0.4)'};
              "
            >
              <svg
                width="14"
                height="14"
                viewBox="0 0 24 24"
                fill="none"
                stroke={isFree ? '#6ee7b7' : '#d4af37'}
                stroke-width="3"
                stroke-linecap="round"
              >
                <path d="M9 6l6 6-6 6" />
              </svg>
            </div>
          </div>
        </button>
      {/each}
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>


========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\TournamentDetail.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount, onDestroy } from 'svelte';
  import { api } from '../lib/api';
  import { auth } from '../stores/auth';
  import { getRealtime, realtimeConnected, TOURNAMENT_EVENT_RE } from '../lib/realtime';
  import { pushNotif } from '../lib/notifications';
  import Avatar from '../components/Avatar.svelte';

  export let tournamentId: number;
  export let onBack: () => void = () => {};
  export let onOpenTable: (tid: number, virtualTableId: number) => void = () => {};

  let t: any = null;
  let loading = true;
  let error = '';
  let acting = false;
  let toast = '';
  let pausedBanner = false;

  async function doPause() {
    if (!confirm('Pause this tournament?')) return;
    acting = true;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/tournaments/${tournamentId}/pause`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Authorization: `Bearer ${localStorage.getItem('admin_secret') || ''}`,
        },
        body: '{}',
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) { toast = '⏸️ Paused'; await load(); }
      else toast = '❌ ' + (data.error || 'failed');
    } catch (e: any) { toast = '❌ ' + e.message; }
    finally { acting = false; setTimeout(() => (toast = ''), 3000); }
  }

  async function doResume() {
    acting = true;
    try {
      const res = await fetch(`/api/admin/tournaments/${tournamentId}/resume`, {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: {
          'Content-Type': 'application/json',
          Authorization: `Bearer ${localStorage.getItem('admin_secret') || ''}`,
        },
        body: '{}',
      });
      const data = await res.json();
      if (res.ok) { toast = '▶️ Resumed'; await load(); }
      else toast = '❌ ' + (data.error || 'failed');
    } catch (e: any) { toast = '❌ ' + e.message; }
    finally { acting = false; setTimeout(() => (toast = ''), 3000); }
  }
  let pollTimer: any = null;

  $: myUserId = $auth.user?.id;
  $: isRegistered = t?.players?.some((p: any) => p.userId === myUserId && p.status === 'active') || false;
  $: myTournamentPlayer = t?.players?.find((p: any) => p.userId === myUserId);
  $: myVirtualTableId = myTournamentPlayer?.virtualTableId ?? null;
  $: canGoToTable =
    !!myVirtualTableId &&
    (t?.status === 'running' || t?.status === 'final_table') &&
    myTournamentPlayer?.status === 'active';
  $: canRegister = t && (t.status === 'registering' || t.status === 'scheduled');
  $: canStart = t && canRegister && t.registeredCount >= t.minPlayers;

  function fmt(n: any, d = 2): string {
    return Number(Number(n || 0).toFixed(d)).toFixed(d);
  }

  function fmtDate(iso: string | null): string {
    if (!iso) return '—';
    try {
      return new Date(iso).toLocaleString('en-US', {
        month: 'short', day: 'numeric',
        hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit',
      });
    } catch { return '—'; }
  }

  function statusColor(status: string): string {
    if (status === 'running') return '#10b981';
    if (status === 'final_table') return '#d4af37';
    if (status === 'registering') return '#22d3ee';
    if (status === 'scheduled') return '#8b5cf6';
    if (status === 'finished') return '#6b7280';
    if (status === 'paused') return '#f59e0b';
    return '#ef4444';
  }

  function statusLabel(status: string): string {
    return status.replace('_', ' ').toUpperCase();
  }

  async function load() {
    error = '';
    try {
      const res = await api.getTournament(tournamentId);
      if (res.ok && res.data) t = res.data;
      else error = res.error || 'not_found';
    } catch (e: any) {
      error = e.message;
    } finally {
      loading = false;
    }
  }

  function onAnyEvent(event: string, ...args: any[]): void {
    const m = event.match(TOURNAMENT_EVENT_RE);
    if (!m) return;
    const tid = Number(m[1]);
    if (tid !== tournamentId) return;
    const type = m[2];
    const data = args[0] ?? {};

    if (type === 'level') {
      pushNotif({
        icon: '⏫',
        title: `Level ${data.level ?? '?'} — blinds up`,
        message: `${data.sb ?? '?'}/${data.bb ?? '?'}${data.ante ? ` · ante ${data.ante}` : ''}`,
        color: '#f4c95d',
      });
    } else if (type === 'elimination') {
      pushNotif({
        icon: '💀',
        title: `${data.displayName ?? 'Player'} eliminated`,
        message: `Position #${data.finalPosition ?? '?'}`,
        color: '#ef4444',
      });
    } else if (type === 'final_table') {
      pushNotif({ icon: '🏆', title: 'FINAL TABLE', color: '#f4c95d' });
    } else if (type === 'finished') {
      pushNotif({ icon: '🏁', title: 'Tournament finished', color: '#10b981' });
    }
    load();
  }

  onMount(() => {
    load();
    pollTimer = setInterval(load, 15000);
    return () => { if (pollTimer) clearInterval(pollTimer); };
  });

  $: if ($realtimeConnected) {
    const s = getRealtime();
    if (s) { s.offAny(onAnyEvent); s.onAny(onAnyEvent); }
  }

  onDestroy(() => {
    const s = getRealtime();
    if (s) s.offAny(onAnyEvent);
  });

  async function doRegister() {
    acting = true;
    try {
      const res = await api.registerTournament(tournamentId);
      if (res.ok) { toast = '✅ Registered!'; await load(); }
      else toast = '❌ ' + (res.error || 'failed');
    } catch (e: any) { toast = '❌ ' + e.message; }
    finally { acting = false; setTimeout(() => (toast = ''), 3000); }
  }

  async function doUnregister() {
    acting = true;
    try {
      const res = await api.unregisterTournament(tournamentId);
      if (res.ok) { toast = '↩️ Unregistered'; await load(); }
      else toast = '❌ ' + (res.error || 'failed');
    } catch (e: any) { toast = '❌ ' + e.message; }
    finally { acting = false; setTimeout(() => (toast = ''), 3000); }
  }

  async function doStart() {
    if (!confirm('Start this tournament now?')) return;
    acting = true;
    try {
      const res = await api.startTournament(tournamentId);
      if (res.ok) { toast = '🚀 Started!'; await load(); }
      else toast = '❌ ' + (res.error || 'failed');
    } catch (e: any) { toast = '❌ ' + e.message; }
    finally { acting = false; setTimeout(() => (toast = ''), 3000); }
  }
</script>

<div class="px-4 pb-28" style="min-height: 100dvh;">
  <!-- ============ HEADER ============ -->
  <div class="flex items-center gap-3 mt-3 mb-4">
    <button
      on:click={onBack}
      aria-label="Back"
      class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95 transition"
      style="
        background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(30,58,138,0.4), rgba(15,31,58,0.7));
        border: 1.5px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.3);
      "
    >
      <svg width="18" height="18" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"
           stroke="white" stroke-width="2.5" stroke-linecap="round">
        <path d="M15 18l-6-6 6-6" />
      </svg>
    </button>
    <h1 class="text-lg font-extrabold flex-1 truncate text-white">
      {t?.name || 'Tournament'}
    </h1>
  </div>

  {#if loading}
    <div class="text-center py-12 text-white/40 text-sm">Loading…</div>
  {:else if error}
    <div
      class="rounded-2xl p-4 text-center"
      style="background: rgba(239,68,68,0.12); border: 1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.35);"
    >
      <p class="text-red-300 text-sm font-bold">{error}</p>
    </div>
  {:else if t}
    {@const sc = statusColor(t.status)}

    <!-- ============ HERO CARD ============ -->
    <div
      class="rounded-3xl p-5 mb-4 relative overflow-hidden"
      style="
        background:
          radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 0%, {sc}30 0%, transparent 60%),
          linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(30,58,138,0.4) 0%, rgba(15,31,58,0.85) 50%, rgba(7,14,27,0.95) 100%);
        border: 1.5px solid {sc}50;
        box-shadow:
          0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.6),
          0 0 30px {sc}20,
          inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.08);
      "
    >
      <div class="absolute -top-16 -right-16 w-40 h-40 rounded-full opacity-20 pointer-events-none"
           style="background: radial-gradient(circle, {sc}, transparent 70%);"></div>

      <!-- Status + id -->
      <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-3 relative">
        <span
          class="text-[10px] font-extrabold px-2.5 py-1 rounded-full uppercase tracking-widest"
          style="
            background: {sc}25;
            color: {sc};
            border: 1px solid {sc}60;
            box-shadow: 0 0 12px {sc}40;
          "
        >
          {statusLabel(t.status)}
        </span>
        <span class="text-[10px] text-white/40 font-mono">#{t.id}</span>
      </div>

      <!-- Buy-in -->
      <div class="flex items-baseline gap-2 relative mb-1">
        <span
          class="text-4xl font-extrabold tabular-nums leading-none"
          style="
            background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8, #d4af37 50%, #a87a15);
            -webkit-background-clip: text;
            background-clip: text;
            -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
            letter-spacing: -0.03em;
          "
        >
          {t.buyIn > 0 ? `$${fmt(t.buyIn)}` : 'Free'}
        </span>
        {#if t.fee > 0}
          <span class="text-[11px] text-white/50">+ ${fmt(t.fee)} fee</span>
        {/if}
      </div>

      <!-- Stats grid -->
      <div
        class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 mt-4 pt-4 relative"
        style="border-top: 1px solid {sc}25;"
      >
        <div class="text-center">
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-1">
            Registered
          </p>
          <p class="text-lg font-extrabold text-white tabular-nums leading-none">
            {t.registeredCount}
            <span class="text-white/40 text-sm">/{t.maxPlayers}</span>
          </p>
        </div>
        <div class="text-center">
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-1">
            Prize Pool
          </p>
          <p class="text-lg font-extrabold tabular-nums leading-none" style="color: #6ee7b7;">
            ${fmt(t.totalPrizePool)}
          </p>
        </div>
        <div class="text-center">
          <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold mb-1">
            Stack
          </p>
          <p class="text-lg font-extrabold text-white tabular-nums leading-none">
            {fmt(t.startingStack, 0)}
          </p>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>

    <!-- ============ INFO ============ -->
    <div
      class="rounded-2xl p-3.5 mb-3 space-y-2.5 text-[11px] font-mono"
      style="
        background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(7,14,27,0.9));
        border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);
        box-shadow: var(--shadow-card), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.04);
      "
    >
      <div class="flex justify-between">
        <span class="text-white/40">Variant</span>
        <span class="text-white/85 uppercase">{t.variant}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="flex justify-between">
        <span class="text-white/40">Format</span>
        <span class="text-white/85 uppercase">{t.format} · {t.speed}</span>
      </div>
      <div class="flex justify-between">
        <span class="text-white/40">Starts</span>
        <span class="text-white/85">{fmtDate(t.scheduledStart)}</span>
      </div>
      {#if t.status === 'running' || t.status === 'final_table'}
        <div class="flex justify-between">
          <span class="text-white/40">Current Level</span>
          <span class="text-gold font-bold">Lvl {t.currentLevel}</span>
        </div>
      {/if}
      {#if t.guaranteed > 0}
        <div class="flex justify-between">
          <span class="text-white/40">Guaranteed</span>
          <span class="text-emerald-400 font-bold">${fmt(t.guaranteed)}</span>
        </div>
      {/if}
    </div>

    <!-- ============ PAYOUTS ============ -->
    {#if t.payouts && t.payouts.length > 0}
      <div
        class="rounded-2xl p-3.5 mb-3"
        style="
          background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(7,14,27,0.9));
          border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);
          box-shadow: var(--shadow-card), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.04);
        "
      >
        <div class="t-label text-gold/70 mb-2">Payouts</div>
        <div class="space-y-1.5">
          {#each t.payouts as po}
            <div class="flex justify-between text-[11px]">
              <span class="text-white/60 font-mono">#{po.position}</span>
              <span class="text-gold font-extrabold tabular-nums">
                {(Number(po.percentage) * 100).toFixed(1)}%
                {#if t.totalPrizePool > 0}
                  · <span class="text-emerald-400">${fmt(t.totalPrizePool * Number(po.percentage))}</span>
                {/if}
              </span>
            </div>
          {/each}
        </div>
      </div>
    {/if}

    <!-- ============ PLAYERS ============ -->
    {#if t.players && t.players.length > 0}
      <div
        class="rounded-2xl p-3.5 mb-3"
        style="
          background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(7,14,27,0.9));
          border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);
          box-shadow: var(--shadow-card), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.04);
        "
      >
        <div class="t-label text-gold/70 mb-2">
          Players ({t.players.length})
        </div>
        <div class="space-y-1.5 max-h-72 overflow-y-auto no-scrollbar">
          {#each t.players as p}
            {@const isWinner = p.status === 'winner'}
            {@const isElim = p.status === 'eliminated'}
            {@const isMe = p.userId === myUserId}
            <div
              class="flex items-center gap-2.5 p-2 rounded-xl"
              style="
                background: {isMe
                  ? 'linear-gradient(90deg, rgba(212,175,55,0.15), rgba(212,175,55,0.05))'
                  : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.25)'};
                border: 1px solid {isMe
                  ? 'rgba(212,175,55,0.4)'
                  : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.04)'};
                {isElim ? 'opacity: 0.5;' : ''}
              "
            >
              <!-- Rank icon -->
              <div
                class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg flex items-center justify-center text-sm shrink-0"
                style="
                  background: {isWinner
                    ? 'linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8, #d4af37 45%, #a87a15)'
                    : 'rgba(0,0,0,0.4)'};
                  border: 1px solid {isWinner
                    ? 'rgba(255,235,170,0.5)'
                    : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.08)'};
                "
              >
                {#if isWinner}
                  <span style="filter: drop-shadow(0 1px 1px rgba(0,0,0,0.4));">👑</span>
                {:else if isElim}
                  <span class="text-xs opacity-60">💀</span>
                {:else}
                  <span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold text-white/50 tabular-nums">
                    {p.seat}
                  </span>
                {/if}
              </div>

              <!-- Avatar -->
              <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full overflow-hidden shrink-0" style="background: radial-gradient(circle, #1a3a5c, #05080f);">
                <Avatar avatarId={p.avatarId || 'king'} size="sm" />
              </div>

              <!-- Name -->
              <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5">
                  <p
                    class="text-[12px] font-extrabold truncate"
                    style="
                      color: {isMe
                        ? '#f4e5a8'
                        : isElim
                          ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.4)'
                          : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.9)'};
                      {isElim ? 'text-decoration: line-through;' : ''}
                    "
                  >
                    {p.displayName}
                  </p>
                  {#if isMe}
                    <span
                      class="text-[8px] font-extrabold px-1.5 py-0.5 rounded-full tracking-wider"
                      style="
                        background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8, #d4af37 45%, #a87a15);
                        color: #0a0800;
                      "
                    >
                      YOU
                    </span>
                  {/if}
                </div>
                <p class="text-[10px] text-white/40 font-mono">
                  Seat {p.seat}{p.finalPosition ? ` · Finished #${p.finalPosition}` : ''}
                </p>
              </div>

              <!-- Stack -->
              <div class="text-right shrink-0">
                <p
                  class="text-[12px] font-extrabold tabular-nums"
                  style="color: {isElim ? 'rgba(255,255,255,0.3)' : '#d4af37'};"
                >
                  {fmt(p.stack, 0)}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          {/each}
        </div>
      </div>
    {/if}

    <!-- ============ ACTIONS ============ -->
    <div class="space-y-2 mt-4">
        <!-- D5.1: Pause/Resume (admin) -->
    {#if t.status === 'running' || t.status === 'final_table'}
      <button
        on:click={doPause}
        disabled={acting}
        class="btn w-full disabled:opacity-50"
        style="padding: 12px; background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f59e0b, #b45309); color: white; font-size: 13px;"
      >
        ⏸️ Pause Tournament (Admin)
      </button>
    {:else if t.status === 'paused'}
      <button
        on:click={doResume}
        disabled={acting}
        class="btn btn-success w-full disabled:opacity-50"
        style="padding: 14px; font-size: 14px;"
      >
        ▶️ Resume Tournament
      </button>
    {/if}

      {#if canRegister && !isRegistered}
        <button
          on:click={doRegister}
          disabled={acting}
          class="btn btn-gold w-full disabled:opacity-50"
          style="padding: 16px; font-size: 14px;"
        >
          {acting ? '…' : t.buyIn > 0 ? `🎟️ Register · $${fmt(t.buyIn + t.fee)}` : '🎟️ Register — Free'}
        </button>
      {:else if canRegister && isRegistered}
        <button
          on:click={doUnregister}
          disabled={acting}
          class="btn btn-danger w-full disabled:opacity-50"
          style="padding: 14px;"
        >
          {acting ? '…' : '↩️ Unregister'}
        </button>
        {#if canStart}
          <button
            on:click={doStart}
            disabled={acting}
            class="btn btn-success w-full disabled:opacity-50"
            style="padding: 14px;"
          >
            🚀 Start Now
          </button>
        {/if}
      {:else if canGoToTable}
        <button
          on:click={() => onOpenTable(tournamentId, myVirtualTableId)}
          class="btn btn-success w-full animate-gold-pulse"
          style="padding: 18px; font-size: 15px;"
        >
          🎰 Go to Your Table
        </button>
        <div class="text-[10px] text-center text-white/50">
          Table {myTournamentPlayer?.tableNumber} · Seat {myTournamentPlayer?.seat}
        </div>
      {:else if t.status === 'running' || t.status === 'final_table'}
        <div
          class="rounded-2xl p-4 text-center"
          style="
            background: rgba(16,185,129,0.12);
            border: 1.5px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.35);
          "
        >
          <p class="text-[12px] text-emerald-300 font-extrabold">● Tournament in progress</p>
          <p class="text-[10px] text-white/40 mt-1">You are eliminated</p>
        </div>
      {:else if t.status === 'finished'}
        <div
          class="rounded-2xl p-4 text-center"
          style="
            background: rgba(107,114,128,0.15);
            border: 1.5px solid rgba(107,114,128,0.3);
          "
        >
          <p class="text-[12px] text-white/60 font-extrabold">🏁 Tournament finished</p>
        </div>
      {/if}
    </div>
  {/if}

  {#if toast}
    <div
      class="fixed top-20 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 rounded-2xl px-4 py-2.5 max-w-[90%]"
      style="
        background: rgba(8,13,36,0.96);
        border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.5);
        box-shadow: 0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,0.7);
      "
    >
      <p class="text-xs font-extrabold text-white">{toast}</p>
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>


========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Tournaments.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount, onDestroy } from 'svelte';
  import { api } from '../lib/api';
  import { getRealtime, realtimeConnected, TOURNAMENT_EVENT_RE } from '../lib/realtime';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';

  export let onOpenTournament: (id: number) => void = () => {};

  type Filter = 'all' | 'running' | 'registering' | 'scheduled' | 'finished';

  let filter: Filter = 'all';
  let tournaments: any[] = [];
  let loading = true;
  let error = '';
  let pollTimer: any = null;

  function fmt(n: any): string {
    return Number(Number(n || 0).toFixed(2)).toFixed(2);
  }

  function fmtTime(iso: string | null): string {
    if (!iso) return '—';
    try {
      const d = new Date(iso);
      const diff = d.getTime() - Date.now();
      if (diff < 0) return 'Started';
      const min = Math.floor(diff / 60000);
      if (min < 60) return `in ${min}m`;
      const h = Math.floor(min / 60);
      return `in ${h}h`;
    } catch { return '—'; }
  }

  const FILTERS: { key: Filter; label: string; color: string }[] = [
    { key: 'all', label: 'All', color: '#d4af37' },
    { key: 'running', label: 'Live', color: '#10b981' },
    { key: 'registering', label: 'Open', color: '#22d3ee' },
    { key: 'scheduled', label: 'Upcoming', color: '#8b5cf6' },
    { key: 'finished', label: 'Finished', color: '#6b7280' },
  ];

  async function load() {
    error = '';
    try {
      const statusParam = filter === 'all' ? undefined : filter;
      const res = await api.listTournaments({ status: statusParam });
      if (res.ok && res.data) tournaments = res.data.tournaments || [];
      else error = res.error || 'failed';
    } catch (e: any) { error = e.message; }
    finally { loading = false; }
  }

  function onAnyEvent(event: string): void {
    if (TOURNAMENT_EVENT_RE.test(event)) load();
  }

  onMount(() => {
    load();
    pollTimer = setInterval(load, 20000);
    return () => { if (pollTimer) clearInterval(pollTimer); };
  });

  $: if ($realtimeConnected) {
    const s = getRealtime();
    if (s) { s.offAny(onAnyEvent); s.onAny(onAnyEvent); }
  }

  onDestroy(() => {
    const s = getRealtime();
    if (s) s.offAny(onAnyEvent);
  });

  function statusColor(status: string): string {
    if (status === 'running') return '#10b981';
    if (status === 'final_table') return '#d4af37';
    if (status === 'registering') return '#22d3ee';
    if (status === 'scheduled') return '#8b5cf6';
    if (status === 'finished') return '#6b7280';
    return '#ef4444';
  }

  function statusLabel(status: string): string {
    if (status === 'final_table') return 'FINAL';
    return status.replace('_', ' ').toUpperCase();
  }

  function formatLabel(t: any): string {
    const map: Record<string, string> = { sng: 'SNG', mtt: 'MTT', freeroll: 'FREE', satellite: 'SAT' };
    return map[t.format] || t.format.toUpperCase();
  }

  // Featured = running tournament with biggest prize pool
  $: featured = tournaments
    .filter((t) => t.status === 'running' || t.status === 'final_table')
    .sort((a, b) => Number(b.totalPrizePool) - Number(a.totalPrizePool))[0] || null;

  $: others = featured ? tournaments.filter((t) => t.id !== featured.id) : tournaments;
</script>

<div class="pb-44 lg:pb-8">
  <TopBar />

  <!-- HEADER -->
  <div class="flex items-center justify-between mt-3 mb-4 px-4">
    <div>
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-extrabold text-white flex items-center gap-2">
        Tournaments
      </h1>
      <p class="text-[11px] text-white/50 mt-0.5">
        {tournaments.length} {tournaments.length === 1 ? 'event' : 'events'}
      </p>
    </div>
    <button
      on:click={load}
      aria-label="Refresh"
      class="w-10 h-10 rounded-2xl flex items-center justify-center active:scale-95 transition-all"
      style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.25);"
    >
      <span class="text-base">🔄</span>
    </button>
  </div>

  <!-- FILTERS -->
  <div class="flex gap-1.5 mb-4 overflow-x-auto no-scrollbar pb-1 px-4">
    {#each FILTERS as f}
      <button
        on:click={() => { filter = f.key; loading = true; load(); }}
        class="px-3.5 py-2 rounded-xl text-[11px] font-extrabold whitespace-nowrap active:scale-95 transition-all uppercase tracking-wider"
        style="
          background: {filter === f.key
            ? `linear-gradient(180deg, ${f.color}, ${f.color}bb)`
            : 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9))'};
          color: {filter === f.key ? '#ffffff' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.65)'};
          border: 1px solid {filter === f.key ? `${f.color}dd` : 'rgba(212,175,55,0.15)'};
          {filter === f.key ? `box-shadow: 0 3px 12px ${f.color}60;` : ''}
        "
      >
        {f.label}
      </button>
    {/each}
  </div>

  <!-- FEATURED -->
  {#if featured && !loading}
    {@const sc = statusColor(featured.status)}
    <div class="px-4 mb-5">
      <button
        on:click={() => onOpenTournament(featured.id)}
        class="relative w-full rounded-3xl overflow-hidden text-left active:scale-[0.98] transition-all"
        style="
          min-height: 200px;
          border: 1.5px solid {sc}60;
          box-shadow: 0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.6), 0 0 40px {sc}30;
        "
      >
        <!-- Background image -->
        <div
          class="absolute inset-0"
          style="background: linear-gradient(135deg, #0a1428 0%, #1e1704 60%, #3a2d08 100%);"
        ></div>
        <div
          class="absolute inset-0"
          style="
            background-image: url('/bg-tournament.png');
            background-size: cover;
            background-position: center;
            opacity: 0.4;
          "
        ></div>
        <div
          class="absolute inset-0"
          style="
            background:
              radial-gradient(ellipse at 100% 0%, {sc}40 0%, transparent 55%),
              linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(5,8,15,0.4) 0%, rgba(5,8,15,0.85) 100%);
          "
        ></div>

        <!-- Content -->
        <div class="relative p-5 flex gap-4 items-center">
          <!-- Trophy image -->
          <div class="shrink-0">
            <img
              src="/trophy.png"
              alt=""
              class="w-24 h-24 object-contain"
              style="filter: drop-shadow(0 8px 20px rgba(212,175,55,0.7));"
            />
          </div>

          <!-- Info -->
          <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-2">
              <span
                class="text-[9px] font-extrabold px-2 py-0.5 rounded uppercase tracking-wider"
                style="background: {sc}30; color: {sc}; border: 1px solid {sc}60;"
              >
                ● {statusLabel(featured.status)}
              </span>
            </div>
            <p class="text-[11px] text-gold/80 font-bold uppercase tracking-widest mb-1">
              Featured
            </p>
            <h3 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-white leading-tight mb-2 truncate">
              {featured.name}
            </h3>
            <div class="flex items-baseline gap-2 mb-2">
              <span
                class="text-2xl font-extrabold tabular-nums"
                style="
                  background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8, #d4af37 50%, #a87a15);
                  -webkit-background-clip: text;
                  background-clip: text;
                  -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
                "
              >
                ${fmt(featured.totalPrizePool)}
              </span>
              <span class="text-[10px] text-gold/70 font-bold uppercase">Prize Pool</span>
            </div>
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3 text-[10px] text-white/60">
              <span>👥 {featured.activeCount || featured.registeredCount}/{featured.maxPlayers}</span>
              <span>💵 ${fmt(featured.buyIn)}</span>
              {#if featured.status === 'running' || featured.status === 'final_table'}
                <span class="text-gold">Lvl {featured.currentLevel}</span>
              {/if}
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </button>
    </div>
  {/if}

  <!-- LOADING -->
  {#if loading}
    <div class="space-y-3 px-4">
      {#each Array(3) as _}
        <div class="rounded-2xl p-4 h-[120px] shimmer"
             style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.6), rgba(11,19,36,0.8)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.1);"></div>
      {/each}
    </div>
  {:else if error}
    <div class="rounded-2xl p-4 text-center mx-4" style="background: rgba(153,27,27,0.15); border: 1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.3);">
      <p class="text-red-300 text-sm font-bold">{error}</p>
    </div>
  {:else if others.length === 0 && !featured}
    <div class="rounded-3xl p-8 text-center mx-4" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.6), rgba(11,19,36,0.85)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);">
      <div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl mx-auto mb-3 flex items-center justify-center">
        <img src="/trophy.png" alt="" class="w-full h-full object-contain opacity-40" />
      </div>
      <p class="text-white/60 text-sm font-bold">No tournaments {filter === 'all' ? 'yet' : `in "${filter}"`}</p>
      <p class="text-white/40 text-[11px] mt-1">Check back soon</p>
    </div>
  {:else}
    <div class="space-y-3 px-4">
      {#each others as t}
        {@const sc = statusColor(t.status)}
        <button
          on:click={() => onOpenTournament(t.id)}
          class="relative w-full text-left rounded-2xl active:scale-[0.98] transition-all overflow-hidden"
          style="
            background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.9) 0%, rgba(7,14,27,0.95) 100%);
            border: 1.5px solid {sc}40;
            box-shadow: 0 6px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.5), 0 0 20px {sc}15;
          "
        >
          <div class="absolute left-0 top-0 bottom-0 w-1.5"
               style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, {sc}, {sc}66); box-shadow: 0 0 12px {sc}80;"></div>

          <div class="absolute -top-10 -right-10 w-32 h-32 rounded-full opacity-20 pointer-events-none"
               style="background: radial-gradient(circle, {sc}, transparent 70%);"></div>

          <div class="p-3.5 pl-5 relative">
            <div class="flex items-start justify-between gap-2 mb-3">
              <div class="flex-1 min-w-0">
                <div class="flex items-center gap-1.5 mb-1.5">
                  <span
                    class="text-[9px] font-extrabold px-2 py-0.5 rounded-md uppercase tracking-wider"
                    style="background: {sc}25; color: {sc}; border: 1px solid {sc}60;"
                  >
                    {statusLabel(t.status)}
                  </span>
                  <span class="text-[9px] font-extrabold text-white/60 tracking-wider">{formatLabel(t)}</span>
                  {#if t.mode === 'free'}
                    <span class="text-[9px] font-extrabold tracking-wider" style="color: #6ee7b7;">FREE</span>
                  {/if}
                </div>
                <p class="text-[15px] font-extrabold text-white truncate leading-tight">{t.name}</p>
                <p class="text-[10px] text-white/45 mt-1 font-mono uppercase">{t.variant} · {t.speed}</p>
              </div>

              <div class="text-right shrink-0">
                <div
                  class="text-[14px] font-extrabold tabular-nums leading-none"
                  style="
                    background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8, #d4af37 50%, #a87a15);
                    -webkit-background-clip: text;
                    background-clip: text;
                    -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
                  "
                >
                  {t.buyIn > 0 ? `$${fmt(t.buyIn)}` : 'FREE'}
                </div>
                {#if t.guaranteed > 0}
                  <div class="text-[9px] text-gold/60 mt-1 tabular-nums">GTD ${fmt(t.guaranteed)}</div>
                {/if}
              </div>
            </div>

            <div class="grid grid-cols-3 gap-2 pt-3" style="border-top: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.1);">
              <div>
                <p class="text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/40 font-bold mb-0.5">Players</p>
                <p class="text-[12px] font-extrabold text-white tabular-nums">
                  {t.activeCount > 0 ? t.activeCount : t.registeredCount}
                  <span class="text-white/40 font-bold"> / {t.maxPlayers}</span>
                </p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p class="text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/40 font-bold mb-0.5">Prize</p>
                <p class="text-[12px] font-extrabold tabular-nums" style="color: #6ee7b7;">${fmt(t.totalPrizePool)}</p>
              </div>
              <div>
                <p class="text-[9px] uppercase tracking-wider text-white/40 font-bold mb-0.5">
                  {t.status === 'running' || t.status === 'final_table' ? 'Level' : 'Starts'}
                </p>
                <p class="text-[12px] font-extrabold tabular-nums" style="color: {sc};">
                  {#if t.status === 'running' || t.status === 'final_table'}
                    Lvl {t.currentLevel}
                  {:else if t.scheduledStart}
                    {fmtTime(t.scheduledStart)}
                  {:else}
                    When full
                  {/if}
                </p>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </button>
      {/each}
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>


========== apps\mini-app\src\routes\Wallet.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';
  import { api } from '../lib/api';
  import { auth } from '../stores/auth';
  import TopBar from '../components/TopBar.svelte';

  let activeTab: 'deposit' | 'withdraw' | 'history' = 'deposit';
  let balance = 0;
  let freeChips = 0;
  let tonStats = { deposited: 0, withdrawn: 0 };
  let hotWalletReady = false;
  let depositInfo: any = null;
  let history: any = { tonTransactions: [], ledgerTransactions: [] };
  let loading = true;
  let error = '';
  let successMsg = '';
  let withdrawAmount = '';
  let withdrawAddress = '';
  let withdrawing = false;
  let walletConnected = false;
  let walletAddress: string | null = null;

  function fmt(n: any, decimals = 2): string {
    return Number(Number(n || 0).toFixed(decimals)).toFixed(decimals);
  }

  async function loadAll() {
    loading = true;
    error = '';
    try {
      const [bal, dep, hist] = await Promise.all([
        api.walletBalance(),
        api.walletDepositInfo(),
        api.walletHistory(),
      ]);
      if (bal.ok && bal.data) {
        balance = bal.data.balance || 0;
        freeChips = bal.data.freeChips || 0;
        tonStats = bal.data.ton || { deposited: 0, withdrawn: 0 };
        hotWalletReady = !!bal.data.hotWalletReady;
      }
      if (dep.ok && dep.data) depositInfo = dep.data;
      if (hist.ok && hist.data) history = hist.data;
    } catch (e: any) { error = e.message; }
    finally { loading = false; }
  }

  async function connectWallet() {
    try {
      const tc = (window as any).tonConnectUI;
      if (!tc) { error = 'TON Connect not loaded'; setTimeout(() => (error = ''), 3000); return; }
      if (tc.connected) await tc.disconnect();
      else await tc.openModal();
    } catch (e: any) { error = e.message; setTimeout(() => (error = ''), 3000); }
  }

  async function doWithdraw() {
    if (!withdrawAddress || !withdrawAmount) return;
    const amt = parseFloat(withdrawAmount);
    if (isNaN(amt) || amt <= 0) { error = 'Invalid amount'; setTimeout(() => (error = ''), 3000); return; }
    withdrawing = true;
    error = '';
    try {
      const res = await api.walletWithdraw(amt, withdrawAddress);
      if (res.ok) {
        successMsg = `✅ Withdraw submitted: ${amt} TON`;
        withdrawAmount = '';
        withdrawAddress = '';
        setTimeout(() => (successMsg = ''), 4000);
        await loadAll();
      } else { error = res.error || 'withdraw_failed'; setTimeout(() => (error = ''), 4000); }
    } catch (e: any) { error = e.message; setTimeout(() => (error = ''), 4000); }
    finally { withdrawing = false; }
  }

  function copyDepositAddress() {
    if (depositInfo?.depositAddress) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(depositInfo.depositAddress);
      successMsg = '📋 Address copied';
      setTimeout(() => (successMsg = ''), 2000);
    }
  }

  function copyMemo() {
    if (depositInfo?.memo) {
      navigator.clipboard.writeText(depositInfo.memo);
      successMsg = '📋 Memo copied';
      setTimeout(() => (successMsg = ''), 2000);
    }
  }

  function useConnectedAddress() {
    if (walletAddress) withdrawAddress = walletAddress;
  }

  onMount(async () => {
    await loadAll();
    const tc = (window as any).tonConnectUI;
    if (tc) {
      tc.onStatusChange((wallet: any) => {
        if (wallet) { walletConnected = true; walletAddress = wallet.account?.address || null; }
        else { walletConnected = false; walletAddress = null; }
      });
      if (tc.connected) {
        walletConnected = true;
        walletAddress = tc.account?.address || null;
      }
    }
  });

  $: tonNet = tonStats.deposited - tonStats.withdrawn;
</script>

<div class="pb-44 lg:pb-8">
  <TopBar />

  <!-- HEADER -->
  <div class="flex items-center justify-between mt-3 mb-4 px-4">
    <div>
      <h1 class="text-2xl font-extrabold text-white">💰 Wallet</h1>
      <p class="text-[11px] text-white/50 mt-0.5">Manage your funds</p>
    </div>
  </div>

  {#if loading}
    <div class="mx-4 rounded-3xl h-[180px] shimmer" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.6), rgba(11,19,36,0.8));"></div>
  {:else}
    <!-- BALANCE CARD -->
    <div
      class="mx-4 rounded-3xl p-5 mb-4 relative overflow-hidden"
      style="
        background:
          radial-gradient(ellipse at 100% 0%, rgba(212,175,55,0.25) 0%, transparent 60%),
          linear-gradient(135deg, #142d52 0%, #0a1a33 60%, #05080f 100%);
        border: 1.5px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.45);
        box-shadow: 0 12px 40px rgba(0,0,0,0.6), 0 0 40px rgba(212,175,55,0.2);
      "
    >
      <div
        class="absolute -top-12 -right-12 w-40 h-40 rounded-full opacity-25 pointer-events-none"
        style="background: radial-gradient(circle, #d4af37, transparent 70%);"
      ></div>
      <div
        class="absolute inset-0 opacity-15 pointer-events-none"
        style="background-image: radial-gradient(circle at 1px 1px, rgba(212,175,55,0.6) 0.5px, transparent 0); background-size: 8px 8px;"
      ></div>

      <div class="relative">
        <p class="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-gold/80 font-bold mb-1">Real Money Balance</p>

        <div class="flex items-baseline gap-2 mb-5">
          <span
            class="text-5xl font-extrabold tabular-nums leading-none"
            style="
              background: linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8, #d4af37 50%, #a87a15);
              -webkit-background-clip: text;
              background-clip: text;
              -webkit-text-fill-color: transparent;
              letter-spacing: -0.03em;
            "
          >
            ${fmt(balance)}
          </span>
        </div>

        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-3 pt-4" style="border-top: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.25);">
          <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-base"
                 style="background: rgba(16,185,129,0.2); border: 1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.4);">
              🎁
            </div>
            <div>
              <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">Free Chips</p>
              <p class="text-[14px] font-extrabold text-emerald-400 tabular-nums">{fmt(freeChips, 0)}</p>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="flex items-center gap-2.5">
            <div class="w-9 h-9 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden"
                 style="background: rgba(34,211,238,0.15); border: 1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.4);">
              <img src="/ton-logo.png" alt="TON" class="w-7 h-7 object-contain" />
            </div>
            <div>
              <p class="text-[9px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">TON Net</p>
              <p class="text-[14px] font-extrabold text-cyan tabular-nums">{fmt(tonNet, 4)}</p>
            </div>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  {/if}

  <!-- TON CONNECT -->
  <button
    on:click={connectWallet}
    class="mx-4 w-[calc(100%-2rem)] rounded-2xl p-3.5 mb-4 active:scale-[0.98] transition-all flex items-center justify-between relative overflow-hidden"
    style="
      background: {walletConnected
        ? 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(16,185,129,0.2), rgba(5,150,105,0.1))'
        : 'linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(34,211,238,0.15), rgba(14,116,144,0.08))'};
      border: 1.5px solid {walletConnected ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.45)' : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.4)'};
      box-shadow: 0 4px 16px rgba(0,0,0,0.4), inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,255,255,0.06);
    "
  >
    <div
      class="absolute -top-6 -right-6 w-20 h-20 rounded-full opacity-25 pointer-events-none"
      style="background: radial-gradient(circle, {walletConnected ? '#10b981' : '#22d3ee'}, transparent 70%);"
    ></div>

    <div class="flex items-center gap-3 relative">
      <div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden"
           style="background: {walletConnected ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.2)' : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.2)'}; border: 1px solid {walletConnected ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.4)' : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.4)'};">
        <img src="/ton-logo.png" alt="TON" class="w-8 h-8 object-contain" />
      </div>
      <div class="text-left">
        <p class="text-sm font-extrabold text-white">
          {walletConnected ? 'Wallet Connected' : 'Connect TON Wallet'}
        </p>
        {#if walletAddress}
          <p class="text-[10px] text-white/50 font-mono mt-0.5">
            {walletAddress.slice(0, 6)}…{walletAddress.slice(-4)}
          </p>
        {:else}
          <p class="text-[10px] text-white/50 mt-0.5">Tap to connect</p>
        {/if}
      </div>
    </div>

    <div class="w-8 h-8 rounded-full flex items-center justify-center relative"
         style="background: {walletConnected ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.2)' : 'rgba(34,211,238,0.2)'};">
      <svg width="14" height="14" viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none"
           stroke={walletConnected ? '#6ee7b7' : '#67e8f9'} stroke-width="3" stroke-linecap="round">
        <path d="M9 6l6 6-6 6" />
      </svg>
    </div>
  </button>

  <!-- TABS -->
  <div
    class="mx-4 rounded-2xl p-1 grid grid-cols-3 gap-1 mb-4"
    style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);"
  >
    {#each ['deposit', 'withdraw', 'history'] as tab}
      <button
        on:click={() => (activeTab = tab as any)}
        class="py-2.5 rounded-xl text-xs font-extrabold transition-all uppercase tracking-wider"
        style="
          background: {activeTab === tab
            ? 'linear-gradient(180deg, #f4e5a8 0%, #d4af37 45%, #a87a15 100%)'
            : 'transparent'};
          color: {activeTab === tab ? '#0a0800' : 'rgba(255,255,255,0.6)'};
          {activeTab === tab ? 'box-shadow: 0 2px 0 #7a560a, inset 0 1px 0 rgba(255,235,170,0.4);' : ''}
        "
      >
        {tab}
      </button>
    {/each}
  </div>

  <!-- DEPOSIT -->
  {#if activeTab === 'deposit'}
    {#if !depositInfo}
      <div class="rounded-2xl p-6 text-center mx-4" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);">
        <p class="text-3xl mb-2">🔒</p>
        <p class="text-white/50 text-sm">Deposits are currently disabled</p>
      </div>
    {:else}
      <div class="mx-4 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-4" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);">
        <div>
          <p class="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/40 font-bold mb-2">Network</p>
          <div class="inline-flex items-center gap-2 px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full" style="background: rgba(34,211,238,0.12); border: 1px solid rgba(34,211,238,0.35);">
            <span class="w-1.5 h-1.5 rounded-full bg-cyan animate-pulse"></span>
            <span class="text-[11px] font-extrabold text-cyan uppercase tracking-wider">{depositInfo.network}</span>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <p class="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/40 font-bold mb-2">Deposit Address</p>
          <div class="flex gap-2">
            <div class="flex-1 rounded-xl p-2.5 font-mono text-[10px] text-white/80 break-all leading-relaxed"
                 style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.06);">
              {depositInfo.depositAddress || '—'}
            </div>
            <button on:click={copyDepositAddress} aria-label="Copy"
                    class="w-11 rounded-xl text-white/70 active:scale-95 flex items-center justify-center text-lg"
                    style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(30,58,138,0.4), rgba(15,31,58,0.6)); border: 1px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.3);">
              📋
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div>
          <p class="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/40 font-bold mb-2">Memo (important!)</p>
          <div class="flex gap-2">
            <div class="flex-1 rounded-xl p-2.5 font-mono text-[12px] font-extrabold text-gold break-all"
                 style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.3);">
              {depositInfo.memo}
            </div>
            <button on:click={copyMemo} aria-label="Copy"
                    class="w-11 rounded-xl text-gold active:scale-95 flex items-center justify-center text-lg"
                    style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(212,175,55,0.2), rgba(168,122,21,0.1)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.4);">
              📋
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>

        <div class="rounded-xl p-3 flex gap-2.5" style="background: linear-gradient(135deg, rgba(212,175,55,0.12), rgba(168,122,21,0.06)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.3);">
          <span class="text-base shrink-0">⚠️</span>
          <p class="text-[10px] text-gold leading-relaxed">
            Send <strong class="text-white">TON only</strong> to this address. Include the memo <strong class="text-white">{depositInfo.memo}</strong> — otherwise your deposit will not be credited.
          </p>
        </div>

        <div class="flex items-center justify-between pt-2" style="border-top: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.1);">
          <span class="text-[10px] text-white/40 uppercase tracking-wider font-bold">Minimum deposit</span>
          <span class="text-[13px] font-extrabold text-gold tabular-nums">{depositInfo.minDeposit} TON</span>
        </div>
      </div>
    {/if}
  {/if}

  <!-- WITHDRAW -->
  {#if activeTab === 'withdraw'}
    <div class="mx-4 rounded-2xl p-4 space-y-4" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);">
      {#if !hotWalletReady}
        <div class="rounded-xl p-3 text-center" style="background: rgba(239,68,68,0.12); border: 1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.35);">
          <p class="text-[11px] text-red-300 font-bold">⚠️ Withdrawals are currently disabled</p>
        </div>
      {/if}

      <div>
        <p class="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/40 font-bold mb-2">To Address</p>
        <div class="flex gap-2">
          <input type="text" bind:value={withdrawAddress} placeholder="EQ..."
                 class="flex-1 rounded-xl p-3 text-sm text-white border outline-none font-mono"
                 style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border-color: rgba(212,175,55,0.2);" />
          {#if walletAddress}
            <button on:click={useConnectedAddress}
                    class="px-3 rounded-xl text-[10px] font-extrabold text-gold active:scale-95"
                    style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(212,175,55,0.2), rgba(168,122,21,0.1)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.4);">
              USE
            </button>
          {/if}
        </div>
      </div>

      <div>
        <p class="text-[10px] uppercase tracking-widest text-white/40 font-bold mb-2">Amount (TON)</p>
        <input type="text" inputmode="decimal" bind:value={withdrawAmount} placeholder="0.0"
               class="w-full rounded-xl p-3 text-2xl font-extrabold text-gold border outline-none tabular-nums"
               style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.4); border-color: rgba(212,175,55,0.3);" />
      </div>

      <div class="grid grid-cols-4 gap-1.5">
        {#each [0.1, 0.5, 1, 5] as v}
          <button on:click={() => (withdrawAmount = String(v))}
                  class="py-2 rounded-lg text-[11px] font-extrabold text-white/85 active:scale-95"
                  style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(30,58,138,0.4), rgba(15,31,58,0.6)); border: 1px solid rgba(96,165,250,0.3);">
            {v}
          </button>
        {/each}
      </div>

      {#if withdrawAmount && parseFloat(withdrawAmount) > 0}
        {@const amt = parseFloat(withdrawAmount)}
        {@const net = Math.max(0, amt - 0.05 - 0.01)}
        <div class="rounded-xl p-3 space-y-2" style="background: rgba(0,0,0,0.35); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);">
          <div class="flex justify-between text-[11px]"><span class="text-white/50">Requested</span><span class="text-white/85 font-bold tabular-nums">{amt} TON</span></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between text-[11px]"><span class="text-white/50">Platform fee</span><span class="text-red-300 tabular-nums">-0.05 TON</span></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between text-[11px]"><span class="text-white/50">Network fee (est.)</span><span class="text-red-300 tabular-nums">-0.01 TON</span></div>
          <div style="height: 1px; background: rgba(212,175,55,0.15);"></div>
          <div class="flex justify-between text-[13px]">
            <span class="text-white/70 font-bold">You will receive</span>
            <span class="text-emerald-400 font-extrabold tabular-nums">~{net.toFixed(3)} TON</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      {/if}

      <button on:click={doWithdraw}
              disabled={withdrawing || !hotWalletReady || !withdrawAmount || !withdrawAddress}
              class="btn btn-gold w-full disabled:opacity-40"
              style="padding: 14px; font-size: 14px;">
        {withdrawing ? 'Sending…' : '💸 Withdraw TON'}
      </button>

      <p class="text-[10px] text-white/40 text-center font-mono">Min: 0.1 TON</p>
    </div>
  {/if}

  <!-- HISTORY -->
  {#if activeTab === 'history'}
    {#if history.tonTransactions.length === 0}
      <div class="rounded-2xl p-6 text-center mx-4" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.15);">
        <div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl mx-auto mb-3 flex items-center justify-center text-3xl" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(212,175,55,0.15), rgba(168,122,21,0.08)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.3);">📜</div>
        <p class="text-white/60 text-sm font-bold">No transactions yet</p>
        <p class="text-white/40 text-[11px] mt-1">Your history will appear here</p>
      </div>
    {:else}
      <div class="space-y-2 px-4">
        {#each history.tonTransactions as tx}
          {@const isDep = tx.direction === 'deposit'}
          {@const sc = tx.status === 'confirmed' ? '#10b981' : (tx.status === 'pending' || tx.status === 'processing') ? '#f59e0b' : '#ef4444'}
          <div class="rounded-2xl p-3.5 flex items-center justify-between" style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(15,31,58,0.7), rgba(11,19,36,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(212,175,55,0.12);">
            <div class="flex items-center gap-3">
              <div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-xl flex items-center justify-center text-lg"
                   style="background: {isDep ? 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(16,185,129,0.25), rgba(5,150,105,0.15))' : 'linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(212,175,55,0.25), rgba(168,122,21,0.15))'}; border: 1px solid {isDep ? 'rgba(16,185,129,0.4)' : 'rgba(212,175,55,0.4)'};">
                {isDep ? '⬇️' : '⬆️'}
              </div>
              <div>
                <p class="text-sm font-extrabold tabular-nums" style="color: {isDep ? '#6ee7b7' : '#d4af37'};">
                  {isDep ? '+' : '-'}{fmt(tx.amountTon, 4)} TON
                </p>
                <p class="text-[10px] text-white/40 mt-0.5">{new Date(tx.createdAt).toLocaleString()}</p>
              </div>
            </div>
            <span class="text-[9px] px-2 py-1 rounded-full font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider"
                  style="background: {sc}25; color: {sc}; border: 1px solid {sc}60;">
              {tx.status}
            </span>
          </div>
        {/each}
      </div>
    {/if}
  {/if}

  {#if successMsg}
    <div class="fixed top-20 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 rounded-2xl px-4 py-2.5 max-w-[90%]"
         style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(6,78,59,0.95), rgba(5,150,105,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(16,185,129,0.6);">
      <p class="text-xs font-extrabold text-emerald-100">{successMsg}</p>
    </div>
  {/if}

  {#if error}
    <div class="fixed top-20 left-1/2 -translate-x-1/2 z-40 rounded-2xl px-4 py-2.5 max-w-[90%]"
         style="background: linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(153,27,27,0.95), rgba(127,29,29,0.9)); border: 1px solid rgba(239,68,68,0.6);">
      <p class="text-xs font-extrabold text-red-100">{error}</p>
    </div>
  {/if}
</div>


========== apps\mini-app\src\stores\auth.ts ==========

import { writable } from 'svelte/store';
import { api } from '../lib/api';

export interface User {
  id: number;
  telegram_id: number;
  username: string;
  display_name: string;
  language: string;
  avatar_id?: string;
}

export interface AuthState {
  user: User | null;
  wallet: { balance_usd: string; locked_usd: string } | null;
  freeChips: { chips: string; total_hands: number } | null;
  token: string | null;
  loading: boolean;
  error: string | null;
}

function createAuthStore() {
  const { subscribe, set, update } = writable<AuthState>({
    user: null,
    wallet: null,
    freeChips: null,
    token: null,
    loading: true,
    error: null,
  });

  return {
    subscribe,

    async init() {
      update((s) => ({ ...s, error: null }));

      const tg = (window as any).Telegram?.WebApp;
      if (tg) {
        tg.ready();
        tg.expand();
        if (tg.setHeaderColor) tg.setHeaderColor('#0a0e1a');
        if (tg.setBackgroundColor) tg.setBackgroundColor('#0a0e1a');
      }

      const tgInitData = tg?.initData;
      let authPayload: string;

      if (tgInitData && tgInitData.length > 0) {
        authPayload = tgInitData;
      } else {
        const params = new URLSearchParams(window.location.search);
        const urlUid = params.get('uid');
        const urlName = params.get('name');

        let devUid: number;
        let devName: string;

        if (urlUid) {
          devUid = Number(urlUid);
          devName = urlName || `User${devUid}`;
          localStorage.setItem('dev_uid', String(devUid));
          localStorage.setItem('dev_name', devName);
        } else {
          const storedUid = localStorage.getItem('dev_uid');
          if (storedUid) {
            devUid = Number(storedUid);
            devName = localStorage.getItem('dev_name') || `User${devUid}`;
          } else {
            devUid = Math.floor(100000 + Math.random() * 900000);
            devName = `User${devUid}`;
            localStorage.setItem('dev_uid', String(devUid));
            localStorage.setItem('dev_name', devName);
          }
        }

        const userJson = JSON.stringify({
          id: devUid,
          first_name: devName,
          username: devName.toLowerCase().replace(/\s/g, ''),
          language_code: 'en',
        });

        authPayload = `user=${encodeURIComponent(userJson)}&auth_date=9999999999&hash=dev`;
      }

      try {
        const res = await api.authTelegram(authPayload);
        if (!res.ok || !res.data) {
          update((s) => ({ ...s, loading: false, error: res.error || 'auth_failed' }));
          return false;
        }

        localStorage.setItem('token', res.data.token);
        update((s) => ({ ...s, token: res.data!.token, user: res.data!.user }));

        await this.refresh();
        return true;
      } catch (e: any) {
        update((s) => ({ ...s, loading: false, error: e.message || 'network_error' }));
        return false;
      }
    },

    async refresh() {
      const res = await api.me();
      if (res.ok && res.data) {
        update((s) => ({
          ...s,
          user: res.data!.user,
          wallet: res.data!.wallet,
          freeChips: res.data!.free_chips,
          loading: false,
          error: null,
        }));
      } else {
        update((s) => ({ ...s, loading: false, error: res.error || 'refresh_failed' }));
      }
    },

    logout() {
      localStorage.removeItem('token');
      localStorage.removeItem('dev_uid');
      localStorage.removeItem('dev_name');
      set({ user: null, wallet: null, freeChips: null, token: null, loading: false, error: null });
    },
  };
}

export const auth = createAuthStore();



========== apps\mini-app\src\stores\cardTheme.ts ==========

import { writable } from 'svelte/store';
import { CARD_THEMES, type CardTheme } from '../lib/card-themes';

const STORAGE_KEY = 'card_theme';

const stored: CardTheme =
  (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined'
    ? (localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) as CardTheme | null)
    : null) || 'classic';

export const cardTheme = writable<CardTheme>(stored);

// Persist whenever it changes
if (typeof localStorage !== 'undefined') {
  cardTheme.subscribe((v) => {
    localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, v);
  });
}

export { CARD_THEMES };
export type { CardTheme };

========== apps\mini-app\src\App.svelte ==========

<script lang="ts">
  import { onMount } from 'svelte';
  import { auth } from './stores/auth';
  import { initRealtime } from './lib/realtime';
  import Login from './routes/Login.svelte';
  import Home from './routes/Home.svelte';
  import Tables from './routes/Tables.svelte';
  import Wallet from './routes/Wallet.svelte';
  import Profile from './routes/Profile.svelte';
  import Table from './routes/Table.svelte';
  import Leaderboard from './routes/Leaderboard.svelte';
  import Events from './routes/Events.svelte';
  import Tournaments from './routes/Tournaments.svelte';
  import TournamentDetail from './routes/TournamentDetail.svelte';
  import Achievements from './routes/Achievements.svelte';
  import Shop from './routes/Shop.svelte';
  import Notifications from './routes/Notifications.svelte';
  import Settings from './routes/Settings.svelte';
  import HelpCenter from './routes/HelpCenter.svelte';
  import Admin from './routes/Admin.svelte';
  import MyTournaments from './routes/MyTournaments.svelte';
  import SideNav from './components/SideNav.svelte';
  import BottomNav from './components/BottomNav.svelte';
  import ToastStack from './components/ToastStack.svelte';
  import Splash from './routes/Splash.svelte';

  type Page =
    | 'home' | 'tables' | 'wallet' | 'profile' | 'table' | 'leaderboard'
    | 'events' | 'tournaments' | 'tournament-detail' | 'achievements'
    | 'my-tournaments' | 'shop' | 'notifications' | 'settings' | 'help' | 'admin';

  let currentPage: Page = 'home';
  let activeTableId = 1;
  let activeTournamentId: number | null = null;
  let tableSource: 'cash' | 'tournament' = 'cash';
  let tablesVariant: string = 'all';
  let tablesMode: 'all' | 'free' | 'real' = 'all';
  let tablesKey = 0;

  $: hideNav =
    currentPage === 'table' ||
    currentPage === 'achievements' ||
    currentPage === 'my-tournaments' ||
    currentPage === 'shop' ||
    currentPage === 'notifications' ||
    currentPage === 'settings' ||
    currentPage === 'help' ||
    currentPage === 'admin';

  let splashElapsed = false;
  $: forceSplash = $auth.loading || !splashElapsed;

  onMount(async () => {
    setTimeout(() => { splashElapsed = true; }, 3000);
    await auth.init();
    const token = localStorage.getItem('token');
    if (token) initRealtime(token);
  });

  function navigate(page: Page) {
    currentPage = page;
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }

  function openTable(id: number) {
    activeTableId = id;
    tableSource = 'cash';
    currentPage = 'table';
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }

  function openTournamentTable(tournamentId: number, virtualTableId: number) {
    activeTournamentId = tournamentId;
    activeTableId = virtualTableId;
    tableSource = 'tournament';
    currentPage = 'table';
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }

  function openTournament(id: number) {
    activeTournamentId = id;
    currentPage = 'tournament-detail';
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }

  function backFromTable() {
    if (tableSource === 'tournament' && activeTournamentId) {
      currentPage = 'tournament-detail';
    } else {
      navigate('tables');
    }
  }

  function quickSeat(variant: string, mode: 'free' | 'real') {
    tablesVariant = variant;
    tablesMode = mode;
    tablesKey++;
    currentPage = 'tables';
    window.scrollTo({ top: 0 });
  }
</script>

<!-- ============ OUTER BACKGROUND ============ -->
<div
  class="fixed inset-0 -z-10"
  style="background: #05080f;"
>
  <div
    class="absolute inset-0"
    style="
      background-image: url('/bg-pattern.png');
      background-size: 600px auto;
      background-repeat: repeat;
      opacity: 0.35;
    "
  ></div>
  <div
    class="absolute inset-0"
    style="
      background:
        radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% -10%, rgba(30,58,138,0.5) 0%, transparent 55%),
        radial-gradient(ellipse at 50% 110%, rgba(212,175,55,0.12) 0%, transparent 55%),
        linear-gradient(180deg, rgba(7,14,27,0.55) 0%, rgba(5,8,15,0.9) 100%);
    "
  ></div>
</div>

{#if forceSplash}
  <Splash />
{:else if !$auth.user}
  <Login />
{:else}
  <div class="relative flex" style="min-height: 100dvh;">
    <!-- ============ SIDE NAV (desktop) ============ -->
    {#if !hideNav}
      <SideNav current={currentPage as any} onNavigate={navigate} />
    {/if}

    <!-- ============ MAIN CONTENT ============ -->
    <main
      class="flex-1 relative"
      class:with-side={!hideNav}
      style="min-height: 100dvh;"
    >
      <div class="mx-auto" style="max-width: 520px;">
        {#if currentPage === 'home'}
          <Home onNavigate={navigate} onQuickSeat={quickSeat} onOpenTable={openTable} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'tables'}
          {#key tablesKey}
            <Tables initialVariant={tablesVariant} initialMode={tablesMode} onOpenTable={openTable} />
          {/key}
        {:else if currentPage === 'wallet'}
          <Wallet />
        {:else if currentPage === 'profile'}
          <Profile onNavigate={navigate} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'table'}
          <Table tableId={activeTableId} onBack={backFromTable} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'leaderboard'}
          <Leaderboard />
        {:else if currentPage === 'events'}
          <Events onNavigate={navigate} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'tournaments'}
          <Tournaments onOpenTournament={openTournament} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'tournament-detail' && activeTournamentId !== null}
          <TournamentDetail
            tournamentId={activeTournamentId}
            onBack={() => navigate('tournaments')}
            onOpenTable={openTournamentTable}
          />
        {:else if currentPage === 'achievements'}
          <Achievements onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'my-tournaments'}
          <MyTournaments onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'shop'}
          <Shop onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'notifications'}
          <Notifications onBack={() => navigate('home')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'settings'}
          <Settings onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'help'}
          <HelpCenter onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {:else if currentPage === 'admin'}
          <Admin onBack={() => navigate('profile')} />
        {/if}
      </div>
    </main>

    <!-- ============ BOTTOM NAV (mobile) ============ -->
    {#if !hideNav}
      <BottomNav current={currentPage as any} onNavigate={navigate} />
    {/if}

    <ToastStack />
  </div>
{/if}

<style>
  @media (min-width: 1024px) {
    :global(main.with-side) {
      margin-left: 240px;
    }
  }
  @media (max-width: 1023px) {
    :global(main.with-side) {
      padding-bottom: 90px;
    }
  }
</style>


========== apps\mini-app\src\main.ts ==========

import '@fontsource/sora/400.css';
import '@fontsource/sora/500.css';
import '@fontsource/sora/600.css';
import '@fontsource/sora/700.css';
import '@fontsource/sora/800.css';
import './app.css';
import './app.css';
import { mount } from 'svelte';
import App from './App.svelte';

// 🔴 TON Connect (optional — fails silently if blocked)
(async () => {
  try {
    const { TonConnectUI } = await import('@tonconnect/ui');

    const tonConnectUI = new TonConnectUI({
      manifestUrl: `${window.location.origin}/tonconnect-manifest.json`,
      uiPreferences: { theme: 'DARK' as any },
    });

    (window as any).tonConnectUI = tonConnectUI;
    console.log('[tonconnect] initialized');
  } catch (e: any) {
    console.warn('[tonconnect] init failed (network?):', e.message);
    (window as any).tonConnectUI = null;
  }
})();

// 🔴 Svelte 5 mount
const app = mount(App, {
  target: document.getElementById('app')!,
});

export default app;
